- **jev-quick**: Fast, low-cost worker for simple, well-specified tasks delegated by jev routing — codebase lookups, small edits, renames, routine shell/git chores, running tests. - **jev-review**: Rerun on 2026-11-10 at executable head `b60b49c`. The earlier subprocess did not inherit `TYPESAFE_API_KEY`; the credential already existed in the interactive shell and macOS app launch environment. Running the installed tools through `/bin/zsh -lic` restored access without printing, persisting or changing the key. This supersedes the earlier claim that a credential needed to be installed. ([JoshuaOliphant/forkhub](https://github.com/JoshuaOliphant/forkhub/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/jev-review.md) · ★1)
- **jev-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer and checker (medium effort) for Jev-routed work: reviews a finished diff for correctness bugs and risky changes. Never edits. ([dansya-arsana/jev-harness](https://github.com/dansya-arsana/jev-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/jev-reviewer.md) · ★2)
